The preliminary experiment does not validate real-flight navigation; rather, it demonstrates that structured semantic geometry can discriminate locations under controlled cross-view perturbations and identifies the harder aliasing, map-aging, and rejection tests required next.

</details>


### [74] [Physics-Guided Biomechanical Gait Adaptation for Humanoid Locomotion on Extreme Sloped Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07830)
*Xuanyu Chen,Mohan Liu,Dengchen Mei,Zhihao Gu,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Haiyue Zhu,Shijun Yan,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumoSlope两阶段框架，通过物理引导的ZMP正则化和生物力学步态适配器，实现盲人形机器人在陡坡（最大62.7%/32.1°）上的鲁棒行走，无需在线外感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模型强化学习在平坦地形上实现了出色的人形机器人运动，但陡坡控制尚未充分探索。斜坡地形带来的持续重力偏差要求同时保持稳定性和姿态控制，通用奖励设计会导致策略收敛到缓慢、保守的低重心蹲伏步态。

Method: 两阶段物理引导框架HumoSlope：Stage I通过地形一致的零力矩点（ZMP）正则化器（在局部倾斜支撑面而非世界水平参考系上评估）建立平衡先验；Stage II引入生物力学斜坡步态适配器（BSGA），利用宏观地形描述符作为特权训练信号，动态门控软奖励先验以根据估计的斜坡几何调整重心高度和下肢协调——鼓励髋主导的上坡推进和膝导向的下坡制动。部署后的执行器完全依靠本体感知，无需在线外感知。

Result: 大量Sim-to-Real实验表明，该框架有效缓解了姿态退化，实现了盲人连续穿越最高62.7%（32.1°）的户外草地斜坡。

Conclusion: 验证了物理引导方法在挑战性斜坡地形适应中的有效性。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning has enabled impressive humanoid locomotion; however, control on steep slopes remains largely unexplored. Unlike flat or discrete terrains, sloped terrains impose a persistent gravitational bias that demands simultaneous stability and posture control. Consequently, under generic reward formulations, policies can converge to slow, conservative low-center-of-mass (CoM) crouched gaits.
  In this work, we propose a novel two-stage physics-guided framework, dubbed HumoSlope, dedicated to robust humanoid locomotion on diverse sloped terrains. Specifically, Stage I establishes a terrain-consistent balance prior by introducing a slope-adaptive Zero Moment Point (ZMP) regularizer evaluated directly on the local inclined support plane rather than a world-horizontal reference. To prevent the resulting policy from defaulting to a crouched posture, Stage II introduces the Biomechanical Slope Gait Adapter (BSGA). Utilizing extracted macroscopic terrain descriptors as privileged, training-only signals, BSGA dynamically gates soft reward priors to modulate CoM height and lower-limb coordination based on the estimated slope geometry -- encouraging hip-dominant uphill propulsion and knee-oriented downhill braking. Crucially, the deployed actor remains entirely proprioceptive, requiring no online exteroceptive sensing.
  Extensive Sim-to-Real experiments demonstrate that our framework effectively mitigates posture degeneration and enables blind, continuous traversal of outdoor grass slopes up to 62.7% ($32.1^\circ$), validating a physics-guided approach to challenging slope terrain adaptation.

</details>


### [75] [Shift & Drift: A Zero-Shot Benchmark for Generalizable and Robust Autonomous Driving Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07844)
*Alessandro Canevaro,Hang Yu,Julian Schmidt,Peizheng Li,Silvan Lindner,Wilhelm Stork,Georg Martius,Julian Jordan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Shift & Drift双轨基准测试，分别评估运动规划器在语义分布偏移（通过航拍数据转换将德国四城及旧金山1,182场景用于零样本评估）和状态分布偏移（注入随机扰动）下的泛化与鲁棒性。结果表明：模仿学习方法在分布内表现优异但在语义偏移和扰动下严重失败，而基于强化学习的方法退化更平缓，揭示了模仿精度与闭环韧性之间的经验权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环运动规划器在大规模目标级数据集（如nuPlan）上表现出色，但在新型城市拓扑结构上的泛化能力及执行扰动后的恢复机制尚未充分探索。

Method: 构建双轨基准：(1) 语义偏移轨道：设计转换流水线将航拍DeepScenario Open 3D数据集转化为nuPlan仿真框架，实现对德国四城和旧金山1182场景的零样本评估；(2) 状态分布漂移轨道：向自车动力学注入随机扰动以量化对累积执行误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 模仿学习方法在分布内基准上得分高，但在语义偏移下（尤其行人密集场景）显著失败，且在时间相关执行噪声下持续漂移；而基于强化学习的方法退化更平缓，在安全性与进度指标上均更优。

Conclusion: 揭示了模仿精度与闭环韧性之间存在经验权衡，为社区提供了评估规划器可靠部署进展的严格基准。

Abstract: While closed-loop motion planners trained on large-scale, object-level datasets, e.g., nuPlan, demonstrate strong in-distribution (ID) performance, their generalization to novel urban topologies and recovery mechanisms following execution perturbations remain under-explored. To address this, we present Shift & Drift, a novel dual-track benchmark designed to rigorously stress-test motion planners across two critical axes of distribution shift: (1) The Semantic Shift Track leverages a novel conversion pipeline that transforms the aerial, DeepScenario Open 3D dataset into the nuPlan simulation framework. This enables zero-shot evaluation of planners trained on North American and Singaporean data against 1,182 scenarios spanning four German cities and the US city of San Francisco featuring dense pedestrian-cyclist interactions. (2) The State-Distribution Drift Track injects stochastic perturbations into the ego vehicle's dynamics to quantify robustness against compounding execution errors. Based on this, we systematically evaluate the failure modes of diverse planning paradigms under semantic and state-distribution shifts. While imitation learning methods achieve high scores in ID benchmarks, they exhibit significant failures under semantic shift, particularly in pedestrian-dense environments, and suffer from persistent drift when subjected to temporally correlated actuation noise. In contrast, the evaluated reinforcement-learning-based planner demonstrates more graceful degradation, maintaining higher safety and progress metrics across both tracks. Our findings reveal an empirical trade-off between imitation fidelity and closed-loop resilience, providing the community with a rigorous benchmark to evaluate progress toward reliable deployment.

</details>


### [76] [Time-to-Collision Based Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance Using Pretrained Vision Models for Robots in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07885)
*Erik Jagnandan,Mulugeta Haile,Gregory Barber,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、数据高效的视觉动态避障方法，利用预训练单目深度估计模型UniDepth和特征匹配管道SuperPoint+SuperGlue，通过计算关键点碰撞时间（TTC）并选择2D运动基元实现避障，在M3ED数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化室外环境中的动态避障对移动机器人仍是关键挑战，现有方法依赖大规模机器人专用训练数据或仿真策略，存在sim-to-real迁移问题且数据效率低下。

Method: 使用UniDepth从RGB视频估计稠密深度图；利用SuperPoint+SuperGlue进行长序列关键点跟踪；结合相机内参和预测深度将2D关键点投影到3D；通过光束法平差优化；计算每个关键点的TTC；选择最小TTC关键点，确定最接近点，然后选择2D地面运动基元使机器人远离该方向。

Result: 在M3ED数据集上，TTC<1秒帧检测精度0.49、召回率0.38；真阳性检测中84%正确生成规避方向；对22个独特物理障碍中的20个成功检测到TTC<1秒的帧；仅需74秒数据进行超参数调优。

Conclusion: 该方法完全消除模型训练需求，仅需少量真实世界数据调参，具有极高的数据效率和良好的可解释性与泛化性，有效避免了sim-to-real问题。

Abstract: Dynamic obstacle avoidance in unstructured outdoor environments remains a critical challenge for autonomous mobile robots, particularly when large-scale robot-specific training data and simulation-based policies are impractical. We present a data-efficient, interpretable method for vision-based dynamic obstacle avoidance that operates entirely on real-world data, avoiding the sim-to-real transfer problem inherent in simulation-trained policies. Our approach leverages UniDepth, a large pretrained monocular depth estimation model, to produce dense depth maps from RGB video without requiring stereo cameras or LiDAR at inference time. Dynamic obstacle avoidance is achieved by extending the SuperPoint and SuperGlue feature correspondence pipeline to track keypoints across long frame sequences, projecting their 2D pixel-space positions into 3D using camera intrinsics and predicted depth, running bundle adjustment initialized from these 3D keypoints, and computing per-keypoint time-to-collision (TTC). A 2D motion primitive in the ground plane is then selected to move the robot away from the closest point of approach of the minimum-TTC keypoint. Evaluated on real-world data from the M3ED dataset, our pipeline achieves a precision of 0.49 and a recall of 0.38 in identifying frames with a ground truth TTC below 1 second, and correctly generates the evasive motion direction in 84\% of true positive detections. Crucially, it detects at least one frame with TTC less than 1 second for 20 out of 22 unique physical obstacles present in our test sequences. Unlike end-to-end learned methods that demand thousands of hours of robot-specific training data, our approach eliminates model training entirely, requiring only 74 seconds of data for hyperparameter tuning. This demonstrates exceptional data efficiency while preserving interpretable and generalizable behavior across diverse obstacle types.

</details>


### [77] [STEMbot: A Compliant Robot for Under-Canopy Plant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07873)
*Zachary Charlick,Nilay Roy Choudhury,Haoyu Ma,Xiaonan Huang,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STEMbot是一种微型攀爬机器人系统，能够在植物冠层下自主导航，利用PIN-SLAM和语义OcTree实现鲁棒定位与建图，通过流形约束A*规划器进行分支感知遍历，可在7-33mm茎干上爬行并检测叶片背面和茎干上的早期害虫，平均Chamfer距离小于1cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机农业的可扩展性受限于害虫监测的劳动力成本。现有空中或地面机器人只能从上方或侧面监测，而许多害虫生活在叶片背面或茎干上，只有在造成显著损害后才能被发现。因此需要一种能够在植物冠层下攀爬微型机器人实现早期害虫检测。

Method: 1) 将全几何PIN-SLAM管道与语义OcTree集成，实现攀爬过程中的鲁棒定位与建图；2) 提出流形约束A*规划器结合射线追踪目标指定，实现分支感知遍历和遮挡目标检测。在四种不同植物标本上，对7-33mm茎干进行实验验证。

Result: 系统可靠穿越7-33mm茎干，在四种不同植物上实现自主导航。平均Chamfer距离小于1cm（相对于离线摄影测量基线），表明全局一致的里程计精度满足自主导航需求。

Conclusion: STEMbot成功实现了微型机器人在植物冠层下的自主攀爬导航，能够到达传统监测手段难以触及的害虫栖息位置，为早期害虫检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The scalability of organic agriculture is partially limited by the labor costs associated with monitoring for pests. While drones and rovers are well-suited for agricultural monitoring from above or next to plants, many pests live on the underside of leaves or on plant stems, making them detectable only after they have caused significant damage. To enable early pest detection we present STEMbot, a miniature climbing robot system designed for autonomous navigation under plant canopies. Unlike existing climbing platforms that lack on-board perception or are restricted to unbranched vertical trunks, STEMbot integrates a fully geometric PIN-SLAM pipeline with a semantic OcTree to achieve robust localization and mapping while climbing the plant. To plan STEMbot's motion we propose a manifold-constrained A* planner along with ray-tracing goal specification to enable branch-aware traversal and the inspection of occluded targets. We validate our system through hardware experiments, demonstrating reliable traversal of stems ranging from 7-33mm and autonomous navigation across four distinct plant specimens. Quantitative evaluations show that our system achieves high-fidelity geometric reconstructions with an average Chamfer distance of less than 1cm relative to an offline photogrammetry baseline, confirming that STEMbot maintains the globally consistent odometry needed for autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [78] [Monocular Vision Based Control Framework for Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07897)
*Shail Jadav,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的单目视觉抓取框架，通过RGB输入和位置控制夹爪，结合开放词汇目标检测、图像分割、边界感知点分配、实时点跟踪和单目深度估计，实现对软硬物体的稳定抓取。核心是基于语言的刚度估计模型，为软物体使用Procrustes不相似度度量追踪形变，为硬物体通过缩放跟踪点距离调节夹爪宽度。在Franka Emika Research 3机械臂上的真实实验中，成功抓取了生菜、新鲜马苏里拉奶酪、羊角面包、纸巾和硬塑料瓶等不同机械属性的物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取方法通常分别处理软物体和硬物体，且依赖触觉传感、物体特定模型或专用夹爪，缺乏统一的视觉驱动框架。本文旨在开发一个仅使用单目视觉反馈即可同时处理软硬物体的通用抓取系统，减少传感器依赖并提高泛化能力。

Method: 框架由五个模块组成：(1)开放词汇目标检测与图像分割；(2)边界感知点分配与实时点跟踪；(3)单目深度估计恢复物体几何；(4)基于语义描述的语言刚度估计模型，推断物体预期柔顺性作为抓取先验；(5)根据刚度估计选择抓取策略：软物体通过Procrustes不相似度度量追踪关键点形变来调整抓取；硬物体通过缩放跟踪点距离来调节夹爪宽度。

Result: 在Franka Emika Research 3机械臂上进行真实拾放实验，使用生菜、新鲜马苏里拉奶酪、羊角面包、纸巾和硬塑料瓶等不同机械属性的物体。实验表明该框架能够仅通过视觉反馈实现软硬物体的稳定抓取。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了一种实用、传感器高效且可泛化的抓取方法，特别适用于食品处理和家庭操作场景，证明了仅使用单目视觉即可统一处理不同机械属性物体的可行性。

Abstract: Grasping in unstructured environments requires handling objects with widely different mechanical properties, from soft and deformable items to rigid everyday objects. Most existing approaches address these categories separately and often rely on tactile sensing, object-specific models, or specialized grippers. In this paper, we present a unified monocular vision-based grasping framework that targets both soft and rigid objects within a single control pipeline, using only RGB input and a position-controlled gripper. The proposed system combines open-vocabulary object detection, image segmentation, boundary-aware point assignment, real-time point tracking, and monocular depth estimation to recover object motion and geometry from visual observations. A key component of the framework is a language-based stiffness estimation model that infers an object's expected compliance from its semantic description and provides an object-level prior for selecting the grasping strategy before contact. For deformable objects, grasp adaptation is governed by a Procrustes-based dissimilarity measure computed from tracked keypoints, which acts as a visual proxy for deformation. For rigid objects, the gripper width is regulated through the scaling of tracked point distances. We validate the proposed method in real-world pick-and-place experiments on a Franka Emika Research 3 arm using objects with substantially different mechanical properties, including lettuce, fresh mozzarella cheese, croissants, paper towels, and hard plastic bottles. Results demonstrate that the framework achieves stable grasping across both soft and rigid objects using visual feedback alone, highlighting a practical, sensor-efficient, and generalizable approach for food handling and household manipulation.

</details>


### [79] [Towards Soft Robotic Exogloves for Musculoskeletal Manipulation to Reduce Pain and Spasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07958)
*Antonia Salluce,Maeryn Erdheim,Gailen Davis,Lauren H. Sullivan,Max-William Kanz,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型软体机器人外手套，通过个性化软体气动执行器同时实现手部运动辅助和按摩式压缩放松痉挛肌肉，初步验证了其舒适性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球1200万人受手部痉挛及相关疼痛困扰，现有软体机器人外手套仅能改善运动能力或管理疼痛，无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 设计了个性化软体气动执行器，包括背侧手指执行器、腹侧手指执行器和手掌执行器；采用3D建模和有限元分析（100和200 kPa压力下）优化几何形状以最小化应力；通过立体光刻3D打印制造；组装成外手套并进行实验测试。

Result: 背侧执行器实现了个性化全贴合接触和最大力分布；腹侧执行器可大幅压缩以适应痉挛手指的狭小空间；手掌执行器成功3D打印，展示了复杂几何形状的潜力；手套成功由试点用户穿戴验证了舒适性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该软体机器人外手套初步证明了同时改善运动能力和缓解痉挛疼痛的可行性，为后续优化和临床研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Hand spasticity and resulting pain affect 12 million people worldwide, including stroke survivors, arthritis patients, and those with other muscle and nerve deficiencies. Soft robotic exogloves are being introduced to help patients enhance mobility or manage pain; however, there are no current solutions that address both pain and mobility. We present preliminary development of a soft robotic exoglove that both aids in mobility and administers massage-like compression to relax spastic muscles. The glove consists of soft pneumatic actuators that are personalized to an individual's hand topology and kinematics, allowing for optimal conformability and targeted mobility. Novel soft actuators were designed, analyzed, fabricated, assembled into an exoglove, and experimentally tested. Actuators were 3D modeled and analyzed with finite element modeling under pressures of 100 and 200 kPa. Geometries were optimized to minimize stress before fabrication and testing. A dorsal finger actuator was successfully customized to a participant's hand topology, providing full conformal contact and maximal force distribution. A ventral finger actuator was successfully fabricated that can be drastically compressed in size to fit into the tight space of a hyperflexed spastic finger. A palmar actuator was successfully printed with stereolithography, showing potential for 3D-printed soft actuators with more complex geometries. The glove was assembled and successfully worn by a pilot user to validate initial findings in comfort and effectiveness.

</details>


### [80] [Soft Robotic Exogloves for Dexterous Mobility -- Towards Personalized Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07968)
*Paul Dela Cruz,Mostafa Mo. Massoud,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于拓扑扫描和硅胶浇铸的个性化软体机器人外手套，通过有限元分析和气动控制实验验证了其在手部康复和辅助中的有效性，实现了对MCP和PIP关节的精准运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机器人外手套依赖标准化尺寸，缺乏个性化适配，限制了对手部精细动作的有效辅助和康复效果。

Method: 采用拓扑扫描获取用户手部解剖数据，通过硅胶模具浇铸制造个性化外手套；利用有限元分析（FEA）评估驱动器弯曲及人机交互接触力；通过气动压力控制实验实现手指静态与动态屈曲控制。

Result: 拓扑扫描可精确适配手部解剖结构；FEA表明个性化建模能分析人机接触力，实现关节有效活动但存在近端指骨非理想压缩；气动测试显示压力控制可精准驱动MCP和PIP关节；放松应变限制层可改善驱动器与手指关节的对齐。

Conclusion: 该工作在结构贴合性、关节拓扑、人机接触建模及时间相关驱动形变方面实现了手部个性化，为外手套设计优化、灵巧操作辅助及精细运动神经肌肉康复奠定了基础。

Abstract: Soft robotic exogloves can provide hand rehabilitation and assistance. Fitting these gloves often relies on standardized measurements not tailored to the individual, limiting their effectiveness, especially for fine articulation necessary for dexterous manipulation. We present the design, fabrication, modeling, and testing of a personalized pneumatically-actuated soft robotic exoglove. The glove was fit to a user's hand with topological scans and fabricated with silicone mold casting. Finite element analysis (FEA) was performed to evaluate actuator bending and forces from physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) between an actuator and a simplified personalized biomechanical finger model. Pneumatic pressure control experiments were conducted to flex the user's finger with static and dynamic references. Fabrication results show that topological scans enable precise tailoring to hand anatomy. Simulations showed that anatomical personalization enables analysis of pHRI contact forces, and results indicate sufficient joint mobilization with non-ideal compression on the proximal phalanx. Pneumatic testing indicates that pressure control allows accurate and targeted mobility of the metacarpophalangeal (MCP) and proximal interphalangeal (PIP) joints with intrinsic stiffness. Testing of multiple designs showed that relaxing the strain-limiting layer improves actuator-to-finger joint alignment during actuation. This work presents personalization to the human hand in structural conformability, joint topology, modeling of pHRI contact, and time-dependent actuation-deformation profiles. This lays a groundwork for informing exoglove design optimization to enable assistance in dexterous manipulation and neuromuscular rehabilitation of fine motor skills.

</details>


### [81] [In vivo feasibility study of humanoid robots in surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07972)
*Zekai Liang,Nikita Thareja,Peihan Zhang,Calvin Joyce,Soofiyan Atar,Florian Richter,Garth Jacobsen,Shanglei Liu,Ryan Broderick,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了现代人形机器人在腹腔镜手术任务中的可行性。通过台式表征、跨不同手术经验水平的干实验室用户研究以及体内猪模型研究，量化了技术可行性、任务性能和临床准备度。结果表明人形机器人在手术应用中既有前景也存在关键技术挑战，在临床部署前还需解决精度、控制和安全性等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域面临人员短缺和护理需求增长的双重压力，而医院工作大量依赖需要移动、操作和交互的具身化任务。虽然手术机器人已有成熟方案（如达芬奇系统），但当前人形机器人能否满足微创手术的精度、控制和安全性要求尚不明确。

Method: 开发了基于人形机器人的腹腔镜远程操作框架，使用通用手术器械。通过三项评估：（1）台式表征量化技术指标；（2）覆盖不同经验水平的干实验室用户研究；（3）体内猪模型实验验证临床可行性。

Result: 系统评估了人形机器人在手术任务中的技术可行性、任务性能和临床准备度，量化了其能力与现有手术平台的差距。

Conclusion: 人形机器人在手术应用中展现出潜力，但在临床部署前仍需解决精度、控制和安全性等方面的关键技术挑战。

Abstract: Recent advances in actuation, control and learning have rapidly pushed humanoid robots from a distant vision towards near-term real-world deployment. Healthcare is a particularly pressing domain, in which staffing shortages and increasing care demand are widening the gap between clinical workload and available skilled labour. Although current automation has largely focused on digital and logistical tasks, much hospital work remains embodied, requiring mobility, manipulation and safe interaction in human-designed environments. Humanoid form factors offer unique potential, particularly for assisting with surgical tasks. Traditionally, robotic systems for surgery are purpose-built platforms such as Intuitive Surgical's da Vinci Surgical System, and it remains unclear how close current humanoid systems are to meeting the precision, control and safety requirements of minimally invasive surgery. Here we present a systematic evaluation of contemporary humanoid technology for laparoscopic surgical tasks. We develop a humanoid-based laparoscopic teleoperation framework using general-purpose instruments and assess its abilities through benchtop characterization, dry-laboratory user studies spanning diverse surgical experience levels and in vivo porcine studies. Across these evaluations, we quantify technical feasibility, task performance and clinical readiness relative to established surgical platforms. Together, our study provides an evidence-based assessment of current humanoid abilities and limitations for surgical applications, highlighting both their promise and key technical challenges that must be addressed before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [82] [D-CLIPSE: Distributed Consensus-based Localization with Passive Listening on Shared State Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07995)
*Kyle Biron-Gricken,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于共识的高效通信分布式滤波框架，通过共享预积分里程计和相关共享状态，在准确性和一致性上接近集中式方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式滤波方法虽能最优融合所有传感器测量，但由于硬件、通信和计算限制难以实际部署；现有分布式方法在一致性和通信效率上存在不足。

Method: 提出基于共识的分布式滤波框架，机器人在通信中共享预积分里程计及相关共享状态，实现一致性约束下的分布式状态估计。

Result: 在仿真和实验场景中验证，该方法在准确性和一致性上均接近集中式方法性能，优于当前最先进的去中心化方法。

Conclusion: 该分布式滤波框架能够在保证通信高效的同时，实现接近集中式滤波的准确性和一致性，为多机器人定位提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot localization that is accurate and consistent is imperative for downstream tasks such as planning and control. Centralized filtering approaches optimally fuse all available sensor measurements of the team. However, a centralized solution is rarely implementable due to hardware, communication, and computational constraints. Distributed approaches deploy a filter on each robot to estimate their own state and neighbours' states using inter-robot communication. This paper proposes a consistent, communication-efficient, and consensus-based distributed filtering framework that shares both preintegrated odometry and relevant shared states among communicating robots. The proposed method is validated in simulated and experimental scenarios, showing near centralized performance in accuracy, and especially in consistency, compared to the current state-of-the-art decentralized approach.

</details>


### [83] [Factors Influencing Conversational Engagement in Robot-Delivered Individual Cognitive Stimulation Therapy (iCST) for Dementia in Home Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07998)
*Emmanuel Akinrintoyo,Nicole Salomons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究分析了机器人在家中为痴呆症患者提供认知刺激疗法（iCST）时的对话动态，发现个性化提示能显著提升参与度，对话行为在会话后期出现认知疲劳迹象，且首次会话指标可预测长期参与情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人在痴呆症护理中具有潜力，但机器人引导的认知刺激疗法会话中的对话动态尚不明确，缺乏对其交互模式的深入理解。

Method: 将Co-STAR系统部署在8名痴呆症患者家中一周，完成每日30分钟iCST会话；分析每个话轮词数、语速、响应时长、响应延迟及自我参照语言等对话指标，考察提示个性化、交互阶段和参与者特征对参与度的影响。

Result: 1）个性化提示显著增加响应时长、自我参照语言和整体参与度；2）会话后期言语输出和自传体参与减少，提示存在认知疲劳；3）首次会话的对话指标与长期参与相关，居住环境影响对话参与模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了影响机器人引导iCST会话参与度的关键因素，为设计适应性的痴呆症治疗对话机器人提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Social robots offer a promising means of supporting cognitive therapies for dementia care by guiding structured conversation and therapeutic activities. However, little is known about the conversational dynamics that emerge during robot-delivered cognitive stimulation therapy (CST) sessions. This study analysed the interaction patterns from robot-delivered individual CST (iCST) sessions conducted with people living with dementia in home settings. Our Co-STAR (Cognitive Stimulation Therapy by an Autonomous Robot) system was deployed in the homes of eight PwDs for one week, who completed 30-minute sessions. Conversational metrics, including words per turn, speech production rate, response duration, response latency, and self-referential language, were analysed to examine how conversational engagement is shaped by prompt personalisation, interaction phase, and participant characteristics. The findings highlight three key interactional properties of robot-delivered iCST. First, personalised prompts significantly increase response duration, self-referential language, and overall engagement compared to generic prompts. Second, conversational behaviour changes within sessions, with a reduction in the verbal output and autobiographical engagement observed during later interaction phases, which suggests cognitive fatigue. Third, first-session conversational metrics were associated with long-term participation, while living situation influenced conversational engagement patterns. These findings provide empirical insights into the factors that shape conversational engagement in robot-delivered iCST. They inform the design of adaptive conversational robots for dementia therapy.

</details>


### [84] [RadLoc: Radar-based 3-DoF Global Localization via Fast, Robust, and Lightweight Spatial Descriptor Across Diverse Environmental Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08115)
*Hogyun Kim,Jiwon Choi,Jungwoo Lee,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RadLoc是一个快速、鲁棒且轻量级的雷达全局定位端到端流水线，从地点识别到3自由度位姿估计，在15个序列的实验中以最小描述符大小和最快检索时间实现了鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于地点识别或位姿估计等单个组件，缺乏一个完整的端到端雷达全局定位解决方案。

Method: 利用1D CA-CFAR滤波加速预处理；基于旋转雷达图像的近距主导特性设计紧凑描述符和分层粗到细检索策略；结合相位相关的3自由度位姿估计，形成适用于SLAM和多会话SLAM的全局定位模块。

Result: 在5个数据集的15个序列上，RadLoc以最小的描述符大小和最快的检索时间实现了鲁棒的全局定位性能，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RadLoc提供了一个完整、高效且轻量的雷达全局定位方案，可无缝集成到SLAM系统中。

Abstract: While global localization using spinning radar has gained attention for its robustness to adverse weather and challenging environments, many studies have focused on individual components such as place recognition or pose estimation. In this paper, we take a holistic view of radar sensor-based global localization and present RadLoc, a fast, robust, and lightweight end-to-end pipeline from place recognition to 3-DoF pose estimation. RadLoc accelerates pre-processing using 1D CA-CFAR filtering and leverages the near-range dominance in spinning radar images to design a compact descriptor and an efficient hierarchical coarse-to-fine retrieval strategy. Moreover, coupled with phase correlation-based 3-DoF pose estimation, it forms a versatile global localization module applicable to SLAM and multi-session SLAM systems. Extensive experiments on 15 sequences across 5 datasets demonstrate that RadLoc achieves robust performance while maintaining the smallest descriptor size and fastest retrieval time among state-of-the-art approaches. The supplementary materials are available at https://sparolab.github.io/research/radloc/.

</details>


### [85] [X-ACTA: eXtended Analytic Center Tension distribution Algorithm for fixed and mobile cable-driven-parallel-robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08265)
*Domenico Dona',Vincenzo Di Paola,Alberto Trevisani,Matteo Zoppi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展解析中心方法，用于在绳索驱动并联机器人的力可行工作空间之外选择绳索张力，该方法保持可微性、包含非线性约束，并确保连续可微的张力曲线、快速实时收敛到唯一解，且将力误差限制在可忽略区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将绳索驱动并联机器人操控到力可行工作空间之外能增强其在激进机动或绳索断裂等挑战场景下的能力。现有张力确定方法很少处理这些场景，因此需要一种能在此类条件下有效选择绳索张力的方法。

Method: 提出解析中心方法的扩展版本，将其作为力可行工作空间之外的绳索张力选择准则，保持可微性并包含非线性约束。

Result: 该方法保持连续可微的张力曲线，确保快速实时收敛到唯一解，与基于松弛的其他方法相比，将力误差限制在可忽略区域。通过帕累托支配分析确认了其在平滑性和力误差方面的优越性，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的扩展解析中心方法在力可行工作空间之外张力选择问题上优于现有方法，兼具平滑性、实时性和低力误差等优势。

Abstract: Steering Cable-Driven Parallel Robots (CDPRs) beyond their Wrench-Feasible Workspace (WFW) augments their capabilities in challenging scenarios such as during aggressive maneuvers or following a cable failure. In this context, although the determination of cable tensions is a well-studied topic, only a few approaches address these scenarios. Therefore, this paper introduces an extended version of the Analytic Center method as a criterion for selecting cable tensions outside the WFW while maintaining differentiability and including non-linear constraints. Notably, the proposed method maintains continuous and differentiable tension profiles, ensures fast real-time convergence to a unique solution, and, in contrast to other slack-based formulations, relegates wrench errors to a negligible area of the WFW. Its superiority in terms of smoothness and wrench error is confirmed via Pareto dominance with respect to the leading state-of-the-art method. Lastly, the effectiveness of the method is demonstrated through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [86] [TFP: Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies for Visuomotor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08283)
*Yushen Liang,Yue Peng,Baosheng Jin,Tianluo Zhang,Xinyu Zhang,Shuyi Zhou,Zhuoran Chen,Xinqi Liu,Shenji Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TFP（时间条件记忆融合策略），为VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型引入轻量级记忆-动作框架，通过液体时间常数动力学维护任务进度信念，并直接调制动作生成，在多项操作任务中提升成功率，尤其在需要阶段依赖推理的遮挡和视觉扰动场景下表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略（如π₀.₅和OpenVLA）本质上是反应式的，即从当前观测、指令和本体状态直接预测下一动作。但在阶段依赖的操作任务中，视觉相似的状态可能因潜在的进度差异需要不同动作，这要求策略不仅具备记忆能力，还需要动态感知的信念更新。

Method: TFP维护一个具有液体时间常数（Liquid Time-Constant）动力学的回合级任务进度信念，并通过自适应调制将更新后的信念直接注入流匹配动作解码器，使时间累积的上下文能直接塑造生成的动作块，而非仅作为被动历史上下文。

Result: 在3.3B参数模型下，TFP在LIBERO上平均成功率从96.9%提升至98.75%，在LIBERO-plus上从91.4%提升至93.77%；在记忆密集型MIKASA ShellGameTouch诊断任务中达到75.0%的成功率。机制分析显示操作事件附近的写增益变化约为非事件阶段的6倍，隐藏状态干预实验表明信念因果地调制生成动作块。

Conclusion: 紧凑的、事件敏感的记忆动力学能够有效提升VLA策略在遮挡、视觉扰动和阶段依赖任务结构下的表现。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) policies such as $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA perform well on many manipulation tasks, but they are often reactive: the next action is predicted from the current observation, instruction, and proprioceptive state. This assumption breaks down in stage-dependent manipulation, where visually similar states may require different actions depending on latent task progress and previous interaction outcomes. We argue that such tasks require not only memory, but dynamics-aware belief updates: the policy should preserve task progress during stable or occluded phases and revise its belief near contact, release, or subgoal transitions. We introduce Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies (TFP), a lightweight memory-action framework for VLA backbones. TFP maintains an episode-local task-progress belief with Liquid Time-Constant dynamics and injects the updated belief directly into the flow-matching action decoder through adaptive modulation. This lets temporally accumulated context shape the generated action chunk, rather than serving only as passive history context. With a 3.3B-parameter model, TFP improves the average success rate from \(96.9\%\) to \(98.75\%\) on LIBERO and from \(91.4\%\) to \(93.77\%\) on LIBERO-plus. On the memory-focused MIKASA ShellGameTouch diagnostic, TFP achieves success up to \(75.0\%\). Mechanistic analyses show that write-gain changes near manipulation events are about \(6\times\) larger than far non-event phases, and hidden-state interventions show that the belief causally modulates generated action chunks. These results suggest that compact, event-sensitive memory dynamics can improve VLA policies under occlusion, visual perturbation, and stage-dependent task structure.

</details>


### [87] [AnyDexRT: Calibration-Free Dexterous Hand Retargeting with Few-Shot Human Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08341)
*Chenxi Wang,Ying Feng,Hongjie Fang,Shangning Xia,Lixin Yang,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyDexRT提出了一种无需标定的灵巧遥操作重定向方法，通过自监督指尖对应学习和少量人类引导，在不同类人灵巧手上实现直观、高效的控制，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作重定向方法依赖手工设计的优化目标、精确标定或全局形状匹配，对手部特异性调参敏感，在不同灵巧手上可靠性差，缺乏通用且直观的解决方案。

Method: 结合自监督指尖对应学习（建立人类手与机器人手之间的语义对应）与少量人类引导（锚定任务相关区域的重映射），并利用接触分类器细化捏合相关的姿态。

Result: 在多种灵巧手和真实遥操作任务上，AnyDexRT提升了重定向质量，减少了手动调参，相比先前方法提供了更直观、高效的控制。

Conclusion: AnyDexRT是一种校准-free、跨手型通用的灵巧遥操作重定向方法，有效平衡了映射的物理可行性与人机直观性，对灵巧操作领域的模仿学习和远程控制具有实用价值。

Abstract: Teleoperation is a key interface for controlling dexterous robotic hands and collecting demonstrations for imitation learning. Its effectiveness largely depends on kinematic retargeting, which maps operator hand motions to feasible and intuitive robot hand motions. Existing methods often require hand-crafted objectives, precise calibration, or global shape matching between human and robot hand spaces, making them sensitive to hand-specific tuning and less reliable across different dexterous hands. We propose AnyDexRT, a calibration-free retargeting method for intuitive dexterous teleoperation across human-like dexterous hands. AnyDexRT combines self-supervised fingertip correspondence learning with few-shot human guidance to anchor the mapping in task-relevant regions, and further refines pinch-related poses using a contact classifier. Experiments on diverse dexterous hands and real-world teleoperation tasks show that AnyDexRT improves retargeting quality, reduces manual tuning, and provides more intuitive and efficient control than prior retargeting methods. Project website: https://chenxi-wang.github.io/projects/anydexrt

</details>


### [88] [SkillPlug: Unsupervised Skill Mining for Few-Shot Adaptation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08354)
*Zi-han Ding,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillPlug是一种即插即用框架，通过自监督技能挖掘从多任务演示中提取可重用的行为基元，仅微调轻量级路由器和动作头即可实现高效的新任务适应，在仿真和真实机器人上均提升了多任务性能和少样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略端到端训练，缺乏显式结构来跨任务重用和重组行为，导致在有限监督下迁移效率低下。

Method: 提出SkillPlug框架，包含技能条件模块和共享技能库；通过自监督目标学习紧凑、可重用、非冗余的行为级基元；技能固定后仅微调轻量路由器和动作头实现任务迁移。

Result: 在两个仿真基准和一个真实机器人上，挖掘的可迁移技能一致提升了多任务性能和少样本适应能力。

Conclusion: SkillPlug提供了一种可扩展的方式挖掘可重用技能，提升了机器人操作中数据高效的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning transferable visuomotor imitation policies that generalize across diverse manipulation tasks and adapt rapidly to new tasks from only a handful of demonstrations remains challenging. Most modern policies are trained end-to-end to map observations directly to low-level actions, offering little explicit structure for reusing and recombining behaviors across tasks and making transfer data-inefficient under limited supervision. We propose SkillPlug, a plug-in framework that augments an existing visuomotor policy with a skill-conditioning module and mines a shared, transferable skill library from raw multi-task demonstrations. SkillPlug learns skills via self-supervised objectives that promote compact, reusable, and non-redundant behavior-level primitives, forming a task-shared prior for compositional control. After skill mining, we keep the learned skills fixed and specialize to unseen tasks by fine-tuning only lightweight router and action head, enabling efficient adaptation without full end-to-end retraining. We evaluate SkillPlug on two simulation benchmarks and on a real robot, and observe that the mined transferable skills consistently improve both multi-task performance and few-shot adaptation. Overall, SkillPlug offers a scalable way to mine reusable skills that improve data-efficient generalization in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [89] [FSD-VLN: Fast-Slow Dual-System Modeling for Aerial Long-Horizon Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08359)
*Xueke Zhu,Qingyan Meng,Liutao Yu,Wei Zhang,Zhengyu Ma,Huihui Zhou,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FSD-VLN快慢双系统架构，解耦语义推理与低延迟飞行控制，在无人机视觉语言导航任务中实现导航成功率翻倍、推理延迟降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法存在全局多模态理解与序列动作生成之间的结构错配，导致长航时空中导航产生抖动轨迹和严重决策延迟。

Method: 提出快慢双系统架构：慢流从预训练视觉语言模型中提取稳定语义先验，快流使用扩散Transformer（DiT）建模跨时间动作分布以生成一致飞行输出；并引入时间感知自适应优化器稳定长序列训练。

Result: 在未见过场景中导航成功率比SOTA方法提升2倍，单动作推理延迟和总任务运行时间降低50%以上。

Conclusion: 验证了解耦语义-控制建模的优越性，为长航时空中视觉语言导航提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables UAV autonomous navigation in unknown environments by mapping language instructions to real-time visual inputs. Compared with GPS-dependent or pre-programmed navigation, VLN supports intuitive human-machine interaction and stronger environmental adaptability, requiring tight integration of high-level semantic reasoning and low-latency flight control.Existing methods suffer from structural misalignment between global multimodal understanding and sequential action generation, causing jittery trajectories and severe decision latency for long-horizon aerial navigation. To solve this issue, we propose FSD-VLN, a fast-slow dual-system architecture disentangling semantic reasoning and low-latency flight command generation.The framework has two asynchronous branches: a slow stream extracting stable semantic priors from pre-trained vision-language models, and a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) fast stream modeling cross-temporal action distributions to produce consistent flight outputs. We further introduce a time-aware adaptive optimizer to stabilize long-sequence training and reduce gradient oscillation.Large-scale low-altitude simulation experiments show FSD-VLN achieves up to 2X higher navigation success rates on unseen scenes than SOTA methods, while cutting single-action inference delay and total task runtime by over 50%. Our work validates the benefit of decoupled semantic-control modeling and provides a practical paradigm for long-horizon aerial VLN.

</details>


### [90] [On Exploring Input Resolution Scaling For Anytime LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08391)
*Ahmet Soyyigit,Shuochao Yao,Heechul Yun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对LiDAR点云3D目标检测的深度神经网络任意时刻计算方法，通过单模型多分辨率推理和截止时间感知调度器，在动态运行需求下实现执行延迟与结果效用之间的权衡，实验表明该方法在nuScenes数据集上显著优于现有方法，并在仿真自动驾驶系统中实现无碰撞导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信息物理系统中，执行延迟与结果效用之间的权衡（即任意时刻计算）已被证明能提升系统性能。本文旨在为处理LiDAR点云的3D目标检测DNN模型实现任意时刻计算能力，以满足动态运行需求。

Method: 提出一种多分辨率推理方法，使处理点云为支柱(pillars)或体素(voxels)的模型能够动态缩放输入分辨率；仅需部署单一DNN模型，避免多个模型冗余；引入截止时间感知调度器，通过准确预测不同分辨率下的执行时间，为每个输入选择最高可行分辨率。

Result: 在nuScenes自动驾驶数据集上，该方法显著优于现有的LiDAR目标检测任意时刻计算方法；在仿真自动驾驶系统中，实现了持续的无碰撞导航，同时避免了因环境复杂性导致的不必要停顿。

Conclusion: 提出的单模型多分辨率推理结合执行时间预测调度的方法，有效解决了LiDAR点云不规则性带来的挑战，为自动驾驶等实时系统提供了高效的任意时刻计算方案。

Abstract: Making tradeoffs between execution latency and result utility (i.e., anytime computing) for adapting to dynamic operational requirements has been shown to enhance the performance of cyber-physical systems. In this work, we focus on enabling anytime computing for deep neural networks (DNNs) that process LiDAR point clouds for 3D object detection. We propose a novel method that enables multi-resolution inference for models that process point clouds as pillars or voxels, allowing the input to be dynamically scaled and processed at the resolution needed to meet timing requirements. Importantly, our memory-efficient approach requires the deployment of only a single DNN model, avoiding the need to deploy multiple models, each trained for a different input resolution. We also introduce a deadline-aware scheduler that selects the highest possible resolution for any given input by accurately predicting the execution time for all possible resolutions at runtime, which is challenging due to the irregularity of LiDAR point clouds. Experimental results on the nuScenes autonomous driving dataset demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing anytime computing approaches for LiDAR object detection. Finally, we deploy our approach in a simulated autonomous driving system, where it consistently enables collision-free navigation while avoiding unnecessary stalls caused by environmental complexity.

</details>


### [91] [EgoWAM: World Action Models Beyond Pixels with In-the-Wild Egocentric Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08436)
*Baoyu Li,Xinchen Yin,Mengying Lin,Yixin Zhang,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoWAM框架，研究世界动作模型（WAM）中不同世界预测目标（Pixel、DINO、3D运动流）对人类到机器人的行为迁移效果，发现DINO和3D流相较于纯行为克隆和像素预测显著提升了策略泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心人类数据为机器人操作提供了可扩展的监督信号，但行为克隆会将可迁移内容（物体、场景、任务语义）与不可迁移因素（人类形态、头部运动、行为风格）纠缠在一起。需要研究世界动作模型是否能通过预测场景演变来提供更好的训练信号。

Method: 提出EgoWAM，一个受控的人-机器人联合训练框架。固定策略骨干网络、动作头和混合数据，仅改变世界预测目标（Pixel、DINO、3D运动流），在三个真实世界双臂任务上比较不同世界表征对迁移效果的影响。

Result: WAM联合训练比行为克隆更有效地利用野外自我中心人类数据。像素预测迁移效果弱，DINO和3D流带来显著提升：DINO将分布外物体和场景泛化提升高达4倍，3D流将域内性能提升20-30%。

Conclusion: 有效的世界目标应抽象外观、捕捉与智能体无关的物理效果、并将相机运动与环境变化分离。DINO和3D运动流作为世界预测目标是更优的选择。

Abstract: Egocentric human data offers scalable supervision for robot manipulation. However, behavior cloning entangles transferable content like objects, scenes, and task semantics, with non-transferable factors like human morphology, head motion, and behavioral style. We study whether World Action Models (WAMs) provide a better training signal by requiring policies to predict not only actions, but also how the scene evolves. The central question is what world representation best enables human-to-robot transfer. We hypothesize that an effective world target should abstract appearance, capture agent-invariant physical effects, and separate camera motion from environment change. We introduce EgoWAM, a controlled human-robot co-training framework that fixes the policy backbone, action head, and data mixture while varying only the world prediction target, comparing Pixel, DINO, and 3D motion flow. Across three real-world bimanual tasks, WAM co-training scales more effectively with in-the-wild egocentric human data than behavior cloning. Pixel-based prediction transfers weakly, while DINO and 3D flow yield substantial gains: DINO improves out-of-distribution object and scene generalization by up to 4x, and 3D flow improves in-domain performance by 20-30%. More details: https://gatech-rl2.github.io/egowam.github.io

</details>


### [92] [Harness VLA: Steering Frozen VLAs into Reliable Manipulation Primitives via Memory-Guided Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08448)
*Yixian Zhang,Huanming Zhang,Feng Gao,Xiao Li,Zhihao Liu,Chunyang Zhu,Jiaxing Qiu,Yuchen Yan,Jiyuan Liu,Wenhao Tang,Zhengru Fang,Yi Nie,Changxu Wei,Yu Wang,Wenbo Ding,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Harness VLA提出了一种记忆增强的智能体框架，将冻结的VLA模型作为可重试的接触式基元，与少量分析性基元组合，通过任务执行轨迹和失败模型学习基元的工作范围，在不微调的情况下将预训练VLA扩展到原始轨迹分布之外，在多种扰动场景下显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端VLA模型在分布内任务轨迹上表现良好，但在部署扰动（如语义重定向、目标重新绑定、空间布局变化、不稳定局部接触）下容易失败；而LLM编码体虽提供语义推理，但纯分析性基元在处理不规则抓取、约束放置和铰接物体交互时存在困难。因此需要一种能够结合两者优势、且无需微调的框架。

Method: 将冻结的VLA模型作为可重试的接触式基元，与少量固定分析性基元（用于接地、暂存、运输、导航、释放）组合；通过任务执行轨迹、全局成功规则和失败模型学习这些固定基元的工作范围；将语义重新接地、非接触执行和VLA重新调度提升到规划器层面，仅保留冻结VLA用于局部接触密集型阶段。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro上提升38.6个百分点，在RoboCasa365上提升25.4个百分点，在RoboTwin C2R上达到58.4%。

Conclusion: Harness VLA通过记忆增强的智能体框架，在不微调的情况下将预训练VLA扩展到更广泛的部署场景，证明了将VLA作为可重试基元与少量分析性基元组合的有效性。

Abstract: Language-conditioned manipulation requires both precise contact-rich control and robust reasoning over language, scenes, and long horizons. End-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong local visuomotor skills, but they are trained on in-distribution task trajectories and often fail under deployment perturbations such as semantic retargeting, goal re-binding, spatial-layout shifts, and unstable local contacts. LLM coding agents provide complementary semantic and compositional reasoning, but purely analytic primitives struggle with irregular grasping, constrained placement, and articulated-object interaction. We present Harness VLA, a memory-augmented agentic framework that exposes a frozen VLA as a retryable contact-rich primitive and composes it with a small fixed library of analytic primitives for grounding, staging, transport, navigation, and release. Rather than expanding the skill library, the harness learns the operating range of these fixed primitives from task-specific execution traces, global success rules, and failure models. By lifting semantic re-grounding, non-contact execution, and VLA re-staging to the planner while reserving the frozen VLA for local contact-rich phases, Harness VLA extends pretrained VLAs beyond their original trajectory distribution without finetuning. Across perturbed tabletop, household kitchen, and clean-to-randomized bimanual manipulation, Harness VLA improves over the strongest relevant baselines by 38.6 and 25.4 percentage points on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365, respectively, and reaches 58.4% on RoboTwin C2R.

</details>


### [93] [FabriVLA: A Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Model for Precise Multi-Task Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08575)
*Shiyuan Yang,Borong Zhang,Jizheng Zhang,Zhijia Tao,Junfei Guo,Donglai Ran,Xu Bian,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FabriVLA是一个轻量级视觉-语言-动作模型，基于1B规模的InternVL3.5视觉语言骨干和流匹配动作头，在Meta-World MT50基准上达到90.0%的平均成功率，证明紧凑型VLA无需数十亿参数也能实现强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖数十亿参数的大规模骨干网络，计算成本高、部署困难。作者旨在探索轻量级VLA是否能在精密多任务操作中达到强性能，降低VLA对超大规模参数的依赖。

Method: 采用InternVL3.5视觉语言模型作为骨干（1B规模），动作头使用流匹配（flow-matching）机制并引入门控自注意力（gated self-attention）处理动作token间的依赖关系，同时融合浅层VLM特征以增强空间上下文信息。整个模型通过单阶段联合优化方式训练，从预训练的VLM和随机初始化的动作头开始端到端微调。

Result: 在Meta-World MT50基准（50种不同操作任务）上，FabriVLA的层级平均成功率达到90.0%，验证了紧凑型VLA在多任务精密操作中的有效性。

Conclusion: FabriVLA表明，基于1B规模VLM的紧凑型VLA能够达到与大规模VLA相当的性能，为构建更高效、更易部署的具身智能操作模型提供了可行方向。

Abstract: We present FabriVLA, a lightweight Vision-Language-Action model for Precise Multi-Task Manipulation. FabriVLA combines an InternVL3.5 vision-language backbone with a flow-matching action head featuring gated self-attention across action tokens and shallow VLM layer fusion for enriched spatial context. The model is trained via single stage joint optimization from a pretrained VLM and randomly initialized action head. On the Meta-World MT50 benchmark spanning 50 diverse manipulation tasks, FabriVLA achieves a tier-average success rate of 90.0%, demonstrating that a compact VLA built on a 1B scale VLM can achieve strong performance without relying on multi billion parameter VLA backbones.

</details>


### [94] [A New Human-Likeness and Comfort Index for Robot Movements Along Prescribed Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08620)
*Rosanna Coccaro,Enrico Ferrentino,Antonio Parziale,Angelo Marcelli,Pasquale Chiacchio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于人类运动对数正态性原理的人形相似度指标，用于在轨迹执行前评估机器人运动的人类相似度，并通过68名受试者的物理交互实验验证了该指标与感知舒适度之间的正相关关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中，机器人仅在外观上类人不足以确保物理交互中的接受度，因为接受度与人类感知的运动舒适度直接相关。现有缺乏在轨迹执行前量化评估运动人类相似度的指标。

Method: 基于人类运动对数正态性原理（lognormality principle），定义了人形相似度指数（human-likeness index），用于对给定末端执行器路径的轨迹时间规律进行先验表征。通过68名受试者的三项物理交互实验验证该指数与感知舒适度的一致性。

Result: 三项实验的结果显示，所提出的人形相似度指数与受试者感知的舒适度之间存在全局一致的趋势，即相似度越高，舒适度越受偏好。

Conclusion: 该人形相似度指数可作为轨迹生成算法在实际执行前评估其产生类人运动表现的有效工具，为提升人机物理交互的舒适度提供先验依据。

Abstract: As human-robot interaction rapidly spreads in numerous fields, the subject of robot acceptance gains increasing importance. Visual similarity to the human body, as occurs for humanoids, is generally not enough to ensure acceptance in physical interaction, as acceptance directly links to comfort and ergonomics, which are measured in terms of the quality of the robot movement perceived by the human. This paper discusses the connection between comfort and similarity of the robot movement to the human one. By considering the kinematic characterization of human movement, this paper focuses on the time laws of such movements, wherein the end-effector path is prescribed. Based on the lognormality principle for modeling human movements, a human-likeness index is defined and used to provide an a priori characterization of trajectories. Such an index can be used to evaluate the performance of trajectory generation algorithms in producing human-like movements before they are actually executed. For validation purposes, 68 subjects are required to judge their comfort. The results of three experimental campaigns involving a physical interaction with a robot demonstrate a globally consistent trend between the preference in terms of perceived comfort and the distribution of the suggested human-likeness index.

</details>


### [95] [Native Video-Action Pretraining for Generalizable Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08639)
*Qihang Zhang,Lin Li,Luyao Zhang,Shuai Yang,Yiming Luo,Shuaiting Li,Ruilin Wang,Junke Wang,Jiahao Shao,Gangwei Xu,Jiaming Zhou,Yishu Shen,Yudong Jin,Fangyi Xu,Shuailei Ma,Jiaqi Liao,Guanxing Lu,Zifan Shi,Yongkun Wen,Yujie Zhao,Weixuan Tang,Xinyang Wang,Chaojian Li,Jiapeng Zhu,Ka Leong Cheng,Nan Xue,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LingBot-VA 2.0，一种为具身智能从头构建的视频-动作基础模型，通过语义视觉-动作分词器、因果预训练、稀疏MoE骨干和异步推理四项核心设计，解决了传统视频生成模型用于物理环境控制时的不足，实现了复杂操作任务的少样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的视频生成模型主要面向数字内容创作，其设计范式（如双向注意力、重构式VAE）不适用于物理环境的机器人控制，亟需一种从底层为具身智能设计的视频-动作基础模型。

Method: (1) 语义视觉-动作分词器：替代传统重构VAE，联合对齐视觉表示与语义、动作；(2) 因果预训练范式：从零训练因果架构，避免双向架构迁移时的灾难性遗忘；(3) 稀疏MoE骨干：扩展模型容量同时保持推理效率；(4) 增强异步推理方案：并行预测未来潜在表示与执行动作，并通过前向动力学基于最新观测重新接地。

Result: 在真实机器人平台上部署验证了LingBot-VA 2.0作为鲁棒基础模型的有效性，在复杂操作任务上展现出少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 针对具身智能场景，从头设计视频-动作基础模型相比直接适配视频生成模型更为有效，四个核心设计原则（语义动作对齐的视觉分词、因果预训练、稀疏MoE、异步闭环推理）是实现鲁棒机器人控制的关键。

Abstract: The advent of video-action models offers a promising path for robot control. Nevertheless, we argue that repurposing video generative models designed for digital content creation is inherently inadequate for physical environments. To bridge this gap, we present LingBot-VA 2.0, a video-action foundation model built from the ground up for embodiment. Four core design principles showcase its evolution from LingBot-VA. (1) Departing from traditional reconstruction-focused VAEs, we introduce a semantic visual-action tokenizer, which aligns visual representations with both semantics and actions, improving instruction following and action precision in subsequent policy learning. (2) Given the strictly causal nature of temporal dynamics, we adopt a causal pretraining paradigm, training from scratch to circumvent the catastrophic forgetting that frequently occurs when adapting bidirectional architectures. (3) To meet the demands of high-frequency inference, our model employs a sparse MoE backbone, expanding model capacity without compromising efficiency. (4) Real-time closed-loop control is realized through an enhanced asynchronous inference scheme, which predicts future latents in parallel with action execution while re-grounding each rollout on the latest observation via learned forward dynamics. Real-world deployment validates LingBot-VA 2.0 as a robust foundation model, as evidenced by its few-shot generalization across complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [96] [Learning Adaptive Solvers for Distributed Factor Graph Optimization on Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08735)
*Jaeho Shin,Maani Ghaffari,Yulun Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeepCORD是一个学习增强的分布式因子图优化框架，通过将并行加速的黎曼优化器展开为可微迭代，学习自监督反馈策略动态调整求解器参数，在一般矩阵李群上实现了自适应同步/异步分布式优化，在SE(3)位姿图优化和SL(4)投影子地图对齐任务中优于现有分布式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式求解器依赖脆弱的经验调参，且主要针对刚体姿态图，缺乏对一般矩阵李群上大规模几何优化问题的自适应分布式求解能力。

Method: 将并行加速的黎曼优化器展开为可微迭代，学习自监督反馈策略，根据优化阶段和通信状态动态调整求解器参数，支持同步和异步两种通信模式。

Result: 在SE(3)位姿图优化和SL(4)投影子地图对齐的真实世界实验中，DeepCORD在大多数基准测试中取得了比现有分布式基线更低的优化目标值。

Conclusion: DeepCORD成功将学习机制引入分布式因子图优化，克服了传统方法依赖手工调参的局限，为多机器人/多会话的大规模几何优化提供了自适应高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern robotic perception increasingly involves large-scale geometric optimization problems distributed across multiple robots or sessions. However, existing distributed solvers often depend on brittle hand tuning and primarily target rigid body pose graphs. To address this, we present DeepCORD, a learning-augmented framework for distributed factor graph optimization on general matrix Lie groups. By unfolding a parallel and accelerated Riemannian optimizer into differentiable iterations, DeepCORD learns a self-supervised feedback policy that dynamically adapts solver parameters according to the optimization phase and communication status. The resulting method enables adaptive distributed optimization over matrix Lie groups under both synchronous and asynchronous communication regimes. Extensive experiments on real-world $\mathrm{SE}$(3) pose graph optimization and $\mathrm{SL}$(4) projective submap alignment show that our method achieves lower objective values than existing distributed baselines on most benchmarks across realistic operating scenarios.

</details>


### [97] [ContactMimic: Humanoid Object Interaction via Contact Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08742)
*Xinyao Li,Xialin He,Runpei Dong,Saurabh Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CONTACTMIMIC是一个学习框架，通过追踪显式的部件级二进制接触指令和关键点轨迹，使机器人能够在人-物交互任务中实现精确的物理接触和接触可控性，无需任务特定奖励即可完成操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠关键点追踪不足以完成物体交互任务（如坐椅子、擦黑板、推家具），因为机器人虽能到达正确姿态却无法建立有意义的物理接触。

Method: 提出CONTACTMIMIC框架，包含：1）追踪显式部件级二进制接触指令与关键点轨迹；2）接触跟随奖励；3）轨迹增强方案以打破关键点轨迹与接触标签之间的相关性。

Result: 在10种人-物交互运动模拟实验中，CONTACTMIMIC展现出接触可控性，无需任务特定奖励即可完成任务，并在接触相关任务上优于仅追踪关键点的方法。消融实验验证了轨迹增强方案的必要性，实物部署验证了5种不同运动中的接触可控性。

Conclusion: 该策略成功解耦了接触行为与关键点几何结构，实现了精确的物理接触以及在部署时按需产生或抑制接触的可控性。

Abstract: Keypoint tracking alone is insufficient for object interaction tasks such as sitting on a chair, wiping a board, or pushing furniture, where the robot can reach the correct pose without making meaningful physical contact with the object. We present CONTACTMIMIC, a learning framework that tracks explicit partlevel binary contact commands alongside keypoint trajectories. CONTACTMIMIC is made possible through the use of contact-following rewards and a trajectory augmentation scheme aimed at breaking the correlations between keypoint trajectories and contact labels. The resulting policy successfully decouples contact behavior from keypoint geometry, and achieves precise physical contact as well as contact-controllability (produce or suppress contact during deployment as desired). Simulation experiments across 10 diverse human-object interaction motions confirm that CONTACTMIMIC exhibits contact controllability that enables it to complete manipulation tasks without task-specific rewards, while also outperforming keypoint-only trackers on contact-relevant tasks. Ablations confirm the necessity of the proposed trajectory augmentation scheme and sim2real deployment validates contact controllability in the real world across 5 different motions. Video results are available on https://lixinyao11.github.io/contactmimic-page/.

</details>


### [98] [DexVerse: A Modular Benchmark for Multi-Task, Multi-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08751)
*Yunchao Yao,Zhuxiu Xu,Tianqi Zhang,Zixian Liu,Sikai Li,Zhenyu Wei,Feng Chen,Dihong Huang,Kechang Wan,Chenyang Ma,Shuqi Zhao,Shenghua Gao,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yi Ma,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexVerse是一个大规模、模块化的灵巧操作基准，包含100个任务、3种机械臂和6种灵巧手，支持视觉变化配置和跨任务/跨本体泛化评估，现有方法在任务泛化和视觉鲁棒性方面面临显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在任务多样性、数据多样性、本体覆盖范围和可控视觉变化方面存在局限，阻碍了跨任务和跨本体泛化的系统研究。

Method: 构建了包含100个任务的模块化基准，涵盖8类操作技能；支持3种机械臂和6种灵巧手；提供可配置的纹理、背景、光照和视角视觉变化；包含VR遥操作接口和3,180条演示数据。

Result: 在19个任务上对Diffusion Policy、DP3、OpenVLA和π0.5等方法进行基准测试，结果显示这些方法在任务泛化和视觉运动鲁棒性方面存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: DexVerse可作为通用灵巧操作的有前景的测试平台，揭示了现有方法在泛化能力上的不足。

Abstract: Building general-purpose dexterous manipulation policies requires benchmarks that go beyond isolated tasks to systematically evaluate policies across diverse interaction modes, sensory conditions, and robot embodiments. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in task and data diversity, embodiment coverage, or controllable visual variation, hindering studies of cross-task and cross-embodiment generalization. We present DexVerse, a large-scale and modular benchmark for dexterous manipulation. DexVerse includes 100 tasks spanning a broad range of manipulation skills, including object grasping and relocation, articulated-object interaction, functional tool use, bimanual coordination, non-prehensile control, contact-rich behaviors, multi-goal execution, and long-horizon multi-stage task completion. It supports 3 robot arms and 6 dexterous hands, and is extensible to new tasks, assets, and embodiments. To evaluate visuomotor generalization, DexVerse provides configurable visual variations in textures, background, lighting, and camera viewpoints. We further provide a VR-based teleoperation interface and 3,180 demonstrations with synchronized proprioceptive, RGB, depth, point-cloud, and state observations. We benchmark representative methods, including Diffusion Policy, DP3, OpenVLA, and $π_{0.5}$, across 19 tasks. Results reveal substantial challenges in task generalization and visuomotor robustness, establishing DexVerse as a promising testbed for general-purpose dexterous manipulation. Project page: https://ycyao216.github.io/DexVerse.site

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Projected Energy Matching for Generative 3D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07749)
*Daniel Barco,Michal Balcerak,Suprosanna Shit,Chinmay Prabhakar,Philipp Denzel,Bjoern Menze,Frank-Peter Schilling*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出Projected Energy Matching框架，通过Helmholtz蒸馏和负缓存策略解决能量匹配中速度场非保守旋度与标量势的结构冲突，实现高效高质3D数据生成，并在医学CT稀疏视角重建中验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量匹配（Energy Matching）将流模型效率与基于能量的模型（EBM）的显式似然结合，但直接在高维3D数据上训练标量势计算成本极高；现有蒸馏预训练流模型的方法中，速度场不可避免包含非保守旋转伪影（旋度），强制严格保守的标量势匹配该场会产生"结构冲突"，降低生成质量和模式覆盖。

Method: 提出Projected Energy Matching框架：(1) Helmholtz蒸馏——利用Hutchinson迹估计器将旋转噪声显式吸收到辅助残差网络中，实现结构松弛；(2) 负缓存（Negative Caching）——跨微批次重用负样本以降低对比训练中的采样成本。整体作为无条件先验用于医学CT稀疏视角重建。

Result: 该流水线将总计算量降至标准能量匹配的一小部分，同时实现高保真重建并成功解决严重测量伪影问题。

Conclusion: Projected Energy Matching通过结构松弛和高效采样策略，解决了能量匹配在高维3D数据上的计算瓶颈和结构冲突，为逆问题提供了可扩展且高效的生成式先验。

Abstract: Energy Matching has emerged as a powerful generative framework that combines flow model efficiency with the explicit likelihood of Energy-Based Models (EBMs) via a single, time-independent scalar potential. However, directly training this potential on high-dimensional 3D data remains computationally challenging. While distilling a pre-trained flow model circumvents some of the initial training costs, we demonstrate that velocity fields inevitably contain non-conservative rotational artifacts (curl). Forcing a strictly conservative scalar potential to match this unconstrained field creates a "structural conflict", which degrades generation quality and mode coverage. To solve this, we propose Projected Energy Matching, a scalable framework that resolves these structural and computational bottlenecks. We introduce Helmholtz Distillation, a structural relaxation that leverages a Hutchinson trace estimator to explicitly absorb rotational noise into an auxiliary residual network. We subsequently refine this landscape using Negative Caching, a memory-efficient strategy that reuses negative samples across micro-batches, rendering sampling tractable during contrastive training with gradient accumulation. We deploy our method as an unconditional prior for real-world medical CT inverse problems, specifically sparse-view reconstruction. Ultimately, our amortized pipeline reduces total compute to a small fraction of that required by standard energy matching, while achieving high-fidelity reconstructions and successfully resolving severe measurement artifacts.

</details>


### [100] [Towards Robust Semantic Video Transmission over Block Erasure Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07823)
*Nargis Fayaz,Homa Esfahanizadeh,Matin Mortaheb,Jinfeng Du,Harish Viswanathan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究语义感知的神经联合源信道编码（JSCC），针对块擦除信道下的鲁棒视频传输，提出了结合空域和特征域设计的神经视频压缩框架。空域采用分块实现局部擦除处理和细粒度鲁棒性控制；特征域对潜在特征进行划分，实现缺失特征的语义恢复。实验揭示了空域JSCC擅长处理随机局部丢失，而特征域JSCC对分布式擦除更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视频传输中块擦除信道的鲁棒性问题，探索语义感知的联合源信道编码方法，分析空域与特征域设计在抗擦除能力上的差异与权衡。

Method: 提出神经视频压缩框架，包含空域分块（支持均匀擦除和两级语义引导的非均匀擦除策略）和特征域潜在特征划分（缺失特征可被语义恢复并保持空间一致性）。

Result: 空域JSCC擅长处理随机局部丢失，特征域JSCC在分布式擦除下更鲁棒且在低损失场景保持保真度；两者存在空域连续性与语义冗余之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 不同域的设计各有优势，需根据擦除模式和任务需求选择，为设计鲁棒、任务感知的视频通信系统提供了指导。

Abstract: This paper investigates semantic-aware neural joint source-channel coding (JSCC) for robust video transmission over block erasure channels. We propose a neural video compression framework exploring both spatial-domain and feature-domain designs. In the spatial domain, video frames are partitioned into blocks, enabling localized erasure handling and fine-grained robustness control via uniform erasure and two-level, semantic-guided non-uniform erasure strategies. In the feature domain, latent features are partitioned, enabling missing features to be semantically recovered while maintaining overall spatial consistency. Comprehensive experiments quantify reconstruction quality under varying uniform and non-uniform erasure probabilities. Our results show that spatial-domain JSCC excels at handling random localized losses, whereas feature-domain JSCC provides superior robustness to distributed erasures and maintains fidelity under low-loss scenarios. The analysis highlights the trade-offs between spatial continuity and semantic redundancy, offering insights for designing robust, task-aware video communication systems.

</details>


### [101] [False Confidence: Automated Labels Confound Fairness Audits in Cervical Spine Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07852)
*Linus Juni,Aasa Feragen,Aditya Parikh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文首次对颈椎MRI分割进行公平性审计，发现使用机器生成的银标（silver labels）而非专家金标（gold labels）作为评估参考会显著高估分割性能（约8个Dice点）并扭曲公平性结论（年龄组差异由不显著变为显著），其机制是通过压缩组内方差（假置信度）而非扩大组间差距（假幅度）。因此，评估分割模型时应使用专家金标，并明确报告参考标签的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的颈椎MRI分割数据集普遍使用机器生成的银标来降低标注成本，但银标本身可能存在偏差，导致公平性审计的结果不可靠。目前尚无针对颈椎MRI分割任务的公平性审计研究，因此需要系统评估参考标签来源对公平性结论的影响。

Method: 使用CSpineSeg数据集，针对性别、年龄和种族三个维度，对比使用专家金标与机器银标作为评估参考时，同一分割模型的性能评分（Dice）和公平性结论（组间差异显著性）的差异，并分析偏差产生的机制（假幅度vs假置信度）。

Result: 模型本身在三个维度上是人口统计公平的；但使用银标替代金标会使Dice评分平均虚高约8个点，并将年龄组的公平性结论从非显著变为显著。该偏差并非来自组间差距扩大（假幅度），而是来自组内方差压缩（假置信度）。

Conclusion: 参考标签的来源是分割评估中的一阶混淆因素，性能和公平性均应基于专家金标报告，任何公平性声明都必须同时说明其参考标签的来源。

Abstract: Automated segmentation of cervical-spine MRI is increasingly used in clinical workflows, yet no fairness audit exists for this anatomy. We show that auditing these segmentation tasks is complicated by a common property of modern segmentation datasets: expert-annotated gold labels are expensive, so abundant machine-generated (silver) labels are added to limit annotation cost. This matters because the reference used to judge a model can itself be biased. In this study, we present the first fairness audit of cervical-spine MRI segmentation across sex, age, and race using the CSpineSeg dataset. We observe that the deployed model is demographically fair, but the choice of reference label, however, is not neutral. Because a dataset's silver labels are generated by a model trained on its gold labels, any new model trained on those same gold labels agrees more with the silver labels than with expert truth: scoring identical predictions against silver rather than gold overestimates performance by ~8 Dice points and turns the fairness verdict for age from non-significant to significant - not by the gap inflation Parikh et al. report (which we term false magnitude) but by collapsing within-group variance (which we term false confidence). Reference-label provenance is thus a first-order confounder in segmentation evaluation: performance and fairness should be reported against expert labels, and any fairness claim stated together with the provenance of its reference.

</details>


### [102] [SCI-Mamba: Unsupervised Learning based Low-Light Image Enhancement for Non-Cooperative Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08033)
*Yiyong Sun,Weihang Shan,Shijun Wei,Diwei Zhou,Guang Zhai*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 针对非合作航天器在轨服务中低光视觉感知的瓶颈，本文提出SCI-Mamba——一种结合自校准无监督学习、线性复杂度VMamba架构和Retinex物理先验的无监督低光增强网络，并构建了Space Dark-1.0数据集，在视觉真实性、色彩保真度和推理速度上优于CNN、Transformer及现有Mamba方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间图像存在严重的低光退化问题，而配对正常/低光空间样本极度匮乏，限制了有监督增强算法的泛化能力，亟需一种适应星载硬件资源受限环境的无监督低光增强方案。

Method: 提出SCI-Mamba，融合自校准无监督学习、线性复杂度VMamba架构与Retinex物理先验，构建轻量化增强管线；同时构建Space Dark-1.0数据集，包含真实轨道图像、暗室硬件在环测量及物理约束合成数据。

Result: 与CNN、Transformer及现有Mamba方法相比，SCI-Mamba在视觉真实性、色彩保真度和推理速度上均表现出优势。

Conclusion: SCI-Mamba为近距离非合作航天器在轨操作提供了一种实用的低光增强解决方案。

Abstract: Low-light visual perception acts as the core visual foundation for on-orbit servicing missions targeting non-cooperative spacecraft, supporting autonomous rendezvous, pose estimation, component detection and robotic capture operations. Spaceborne imagery suffers from severe low-light degradation, while the extreme scarcity of paired normal/low-light space samples severely limits the generalization capacity of supervised enhancement algorithms. To address this practical bottleneck, this paper proposes SCI-Mamba, an unsupervised enhancement network for low-light orbital spacecraft observations. The proposed framework unites self-calibrated unsupervised learning, linear-complexity VMamba architecture and Retinex physical priors, delivering a lightweight enhancement pipeline adaptable to resource-limited spaceborne hardware. We construct Space Dark-1.0, a dedicated low-light spacecraft dataset integrating real orbital footage, darkroom hardware-in-the-loop measurements and physically constrained synthetic data covering diverse illumination, motion and attitude conditions. Comprehensive comparisons with CNN-, Transformer- and prevailing Mamba-based approaches verify the advantages of SCI-Mamba in visual authenticity, color fidelity and inference speed. The proposed framework provides a practical low-light enhancement solution for close-proximity non-cooperative space operations.
  The code is available at https://github.com/bitswh/SCI-Mamba

</details>


### [103] [ConRad: Efficient Conformal Prediction for Radiomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08084)
*Matt Y. Cheung,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ConRad框架，利用预测掩膜、输入图像、预测影像组学和边界不确定性等协变量构建自适应保形预测区间，在保持覆盖的同时提高影像组学特征效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影像组学特征常从预测分割掩膜计算，但分割模型可能过度自信或校准不良，导致测量结果看似比实际更可靠。现有保形预测方法对分割衍生影像组学的黑箱区间效率低下，因为忽略了测试时图像外观、掩膜几何形状和分割不确定性等信息。

Method: 提出ConRad框架，使用预测掩膜、输入图像、预测影像组学和边界不确定性导出的协变量，构建自适应保形预测区间，在保持覆盖的同时提高区间效率。

Result: 在五个2D医学影像数据集和171个保留影像组学目标上，ConRad相比基线方法提高了特征级效率，同时保持接近名义经验覆盖率。消融实验表明分割边界不确定性特征对区间效率贡献最大。

Conclusion: ConRad通过利用协变量信息构建自适应区间，有效解决了分割衍生影像组学特征的不确定性量化问题，其中边界不确定性信息对提升区间效率最为关键。

Abstract: Radiomic features derived from medical images and segmentation masks are used to support decision making in clinical imaging pipelines. In practice, these features are often computed from predicted masks, but segmentation models can be overconfident or poorly calibrated, making derived measurements appear more reliable than they are. Conformal prediction (CP) provides distribution-free prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees, but black-box intervals for segmentation-derived radiomics can be inefficient because they ignore test-time information about image appearance, mask geometry, and segmentation uncertainty. We propose ConRad, a conformal framework for scalar radiomic targets that uses covariates derived from the predicted mask, input image, predicted radiomics, and boundary uncertainty to construct adaptive intervals while maintaining coverage. Across five 2D medical imaging datasets and 171 retained radiomic targets, we show that ConRad improves feature-level efficiency compared to baselines while maintaining near-nominal empirical coverage. Ablation results further indicate that segmentation boundary uncertainty features are the largest contributors to interval efficiency.

</details>


### [104] [Partial-Reference IQA Based on Hermite-Gauss Structural Prediction and Texture Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08563)
*Paolo Giannitrapani,Elio D. Di Claudio,Giovanni Jacovitti*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: PreSPA是一种部分参考图像质量评估框架，通过结构感知指数（无参考）和纹理敏感指数（仅从参考图像提取单个标量先验μ）分解感知质量，经三参数仿射融合输出最终分数，在六个基准测试中性能媲美甚至超越全参考模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像质量评估方法中，全参考模型依赖完整参考图像导致高带宽开销，而无参考模型精度不足。作者希望在最小化参考信息量的前提下，实现接近全参考模型的评估精度。

Method: 将感知质量分解为两个互补指标：1）结构感知指标（无参考方式），通过Hermite-Gauss预测失真梯度场及其曲率角度方差来捕捉结构退化；2）纹理敏感指标，从参考图像中提取单个标量先验μ（强边缘区域能量差与弱结构区域累积），估计局部噪声。最终通过三参数仿射融合输出分数，观测距离嵌入算子尺度。

Result: 在六个标准基准测试上的广泛评估表明，PreSPA持续媲美或超越领先的无参考方法，在某些情况下甚至匹配全参考模型的精度。

Conclusion: 通过将参考信息压缩至信息论最小量（单个标量μ），PreSPA实现了紧凑、透明、计算高效且无需数据集特定校准的IQA框架，在精度与参考开销之间取得了优异平衡。

Abstract: We propose PreSPA (Partial-Reference Structural Prediction Approach), a Partial-Reference Image Quality Assessment framework that decomposes perceptual quality into two complementary indices. A structure-aware index, operating in a No-Reference manner, captures structural degradation through Hermite-Gauss prediction of the distorted gradient field and the angular variance of its curvature. A texture-sensitive index estimates local noise through a scalar prior $μ$, obtained from energy differences between reference and distorted complex gradient maps on strong-edge regions and accumulated over weakly-structured ones, reflecting the perceptual leakage of degraded edges into surrounding textures. Crucially, $μ$ is the only information extracted from the reference and is computed once per image pair, reducing the reference footprint to its information-theoretic minimum. The final score is produced by an affine fusion with only three interpretable parameters, making the method compact, transparent, and computationally efficient, with the viewing distance embedded into the operator scale and no dataset-specific calibration. Extensive evaluations on six standard benchmarks show that PreSPA consistently rivals or exceeds leading No-Reference approaches, while in several cases matching the accuracy of Full-Reference models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于上下文图（Context Graph）的主动式智能体框架，通过实时监控企业实体状态变化、计算主动评分并利用LLM进行可解释通知推送，将信息主动呈现在用户查询之前。在合同管理、工程响应和销售管道三个场景中，Precision@5达0.83，误报率0.11，从被动响应的47分钟缩短至30秒以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和智能体系统本质上是**被动响应式**的，必须等待人类查询才能行动。论文认为，真正的企业生产力提升需要**主动式智能体**——在用户提问之前就将相关信息推送给工作者。

Method: 1) **上下文图（Context Graph）**：用NetworkX构建的实时关系数据结构，建模企业实体、关系及状态随时间的变化；2) **Delta检测引擎**：持续监控状态变更；3) **主动评分器（Proactivity Scorer）**：根据紧迫性、相关性和角色匹配度对候选洞察进行排序，定义统一的主动评分函数；4) **展示层（Surfacing Layer）**：基于LLM（Anthropic Claude API）生成带可理解解释的排名通知。

Result: 在合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应和销售管道健康三个企业案例中，Precision@5达到0.83，误报率（FPR）为0.11，平均信息呈现时间从被动基线的47分钟降至30秒以内。

Conclusion: 基于上下文图的主动式方法能显著提升企业智能体的响应效率，将等待时间从分钟级压缩到秒级，同时保持较高的精确度和较低的误报率，是推动企业AI从被动响应走向主动服务的关键技术路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>


### [106] [AI-integrated models for assessing agricultural resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07759)
*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发了一种AI驱动工具，整合经济模型(GTAP)与生物物理模型(APSIM)，通过自然语言查询分析农业供应链冲击的跨学科影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业供应链易受生物物理与经济系统耦合扰动的影响，现有方法难以跨学科整合评估冲击效应。

Method: 将全球贸易分析模型(GTAP)与农业生产系统模型(APSIM)集成，结合AI技术实现自然语言交互的跨学科影响分析。

Result: 构建了支持自然语言查询的AI工具，使政策制定者和市场参与者能够评估供应链冲击的跨学科影响。

Conclusion: 该AI工具有效弥合了经济与生物物理模型之间的鸿沟，提升了农业供应链冲击分析的可及性和综合性。

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

</details>


### [107] [Adversarial Social Epistemology for Assemblies of Humans and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07760)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对抗性社会认识论（ASE），用于分析密集互动型传播环境中，行为者如何利用通常使断言可信的承诺与权利来扭曲、操纵信息，并提供了审计和修复信任破坏的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（如信息茧房、回音室、 misinformation 传播）不足以充分解释行为者如何利用使链条式断言可信的规范机制来操纵信息以获取私利。

Method: 基于推理主义语义学，结合认识论网络，构建对抗性社会认识论框架，分析行为者如何破坏断言的可审计性，并提出相应的审计与补救机制。

Result: 提供了分析对抗性传播的语言工具，识别了信任破坏的机制，并建立了可审计性破坏的审计与补救框架。

Conclusion: 对抗性社会认识论能更好地解释密集传播环境中信息操纵的系统性特征，并为修复信任提供理论工具。

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [108] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述从临床实践和计算方法双视角审视医学大语言模型进展，基于Miller金字塔建立五级能力框架，将演绎、归纳、溯因推理模式与医疗任务关联，并通过18个模型的基准测试揭示专科模型在诊断任务中占优、通用模型在决策支持与对话中领先，最后讨论了数据限制、幻觉和落地等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗大语言模型发展迅速，但缺乏从临床实践和计算方法双重视角系统审视推理能力的研究。现有评估往往忽视临床推理的分级特性和多种推理模式的互补关系，亟需建立连接临床需求与技术方案的统一框架。

Method: 采用双视角分析方法：临床侧基于Miller金字塔建立从知识回忆到动态病例管理的五级能力体系；计算侧将演绎推理、归纳推理和溯因推理模式映射到常见医疗目标和任务。构建覆盖五级医疗推理能力的基准数据集，在18个最先进模型上进行评估。

Result: 医学专科模型在以诊断为中心的任务中表现优异，而通用模型在决策支持和对话任务中领先。揭示了当前模型在不同推理层级上的性能差异，以及专业化和通用化路线各自的优势领域。

Conclusion: 尽管医疗LLM取得显著进展，仍面临数据限制、幻觉问题和事实基础不足等挑战。未来需要朝着更安全、更可靠、更贴近临床工作流程的方向发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [109] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLMs用于心理健康支持时，需通过三层对齐（价值规范、嵌入训练、部署监督）构建"对齐可信度"，类比生物可信度作为监管框架，确保AI系统对患者无害且有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs已成为心理健康支持的重要提供者，但它们是注意力经济的产物，其运营和商业目标偏向持续参与而非有效心理支持所需的摩擦。开发者的安全响应多为被动应对，关注最明显、最急性的伤害，而更隐蔽、长期的危害模式（如依赖、边界侵蚀、扭曲信念放大）则较少受到关注。

Method: 提出使LLMs在结构上安全的三层对齐框架，模仿人类社会对临床实践安全的保障方式：1) 以临床实践的规范性承诺为基础进行明确价值规范；2) 通过训练将这些价值嵌入模型；3) 在部署期间进行监督以检测漂移和长期危害。由此构建"对齐可信度"概念。

Result: 提出了"对齐可信度"这一监管构念，类比生物可信度，作为评估AI系统是否与积极健康结果对齐、即使有能力也不会造成伤害、并能最终使患者受益的结构化论证方式。

Conclusion: 对齐可信度可作为AI健康领域的监管构念，提供一种有原则的方式来判断是否信任AI系统已与积极健康结果对齐、不会造成伤害并最终使患者受益。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>


### [110] [Idiobionics: The Unification of Privacy and Intelligent Robotic Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07775)
*Kwesi Afari Darfoor,Patrick M. Pilarski,Bailey Kacsmar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"idiobionics"这一新的研究方向，系统研究智能仿生肢体的隐私风险与安全问题，通过展示潜在对抗攻击并列出开放研究问题，旨在推动可穿戴机器人领域隐私保护研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能仿生肢体的传感和控制进步在提升能力的同时引入了可被恶意利用的威胁向量，可能侵犯用户隐私。为充分实现下一代仿生肢体的益处，需直接理解并解决这些隐私风险及其对用户采纳的障碍。

Method: 定义idiobionics概念并将其扎根于相关文献；通过初步证据展示和讨论可能利用智能仿生肢体设计的潜在对抗攻击；整理一份面向可穿戴机器人及其他人类交互自主系统研究人员的开放研究问题清单。

Result: 提出了idiobionics这一新研究方向；展示了潜在对抗攻击的初步证据；整理了一份开放研究问题清单。

Conclusion: Idiobionics研究将有助于释放机器人假肢及相关仿生设备的全部潜力。

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users.
